Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a systematics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 756. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 970/4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by systematics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 756, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 970. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The systematics variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 250 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 356 and intersection form of rank 3.
